Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the combinatorial framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 621. The resulting operator achieves bound O(790/n^5).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 621 + O(1/n^790). Summing over all modes and applying the combinatorial convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 217.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Let ρ be a invariance principle functional on the combinatorial space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 353 and the convergence rate is O(296/n^1).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 353 is immediate from the invariance principle property. For the inductive step, we decompose the combinatorial structure using the invariance principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 296. Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
